Cape Cod Cannabis Recreational Dispensary is an adult-use cannabis dispensary located on Route 6 in South Wellfleet, proudly serving residents and visitors throughout Cape Cod, including:

Barnstable • Bourne • Brewster • Chatham • Dennis • Eastham • Falmouth • Harwich • Hyannis • Mashpee • Orleans • Provincetown • Sandwich • Truro • Wellfleet • Yarmouth

Cape Cod Cannabis Recreational Dispensary is a proud member of the following Chambers on Cape Cod:

Wellfleet Chamber of Commerce • Eastham Chamber of Commerce • Yarmouth Chamber of Commerce
